The critique is complete and written to `reports/deepseek_es_corroboration_prereg_2026-10-26.md` (109 lines, no leftover markers). I read only — no code executed, no DB run, no source file edited.

## What I did

Read the ES prereg (incl. Amendment 1), the mechanism prereg, my earlier mechanism review, and — crucially — the *actual implementation* the prereg doesn't mention: `migrate_create_price_bars_es_20261026.mjs`, `load_es_bars.mjs`, `verify_es_bars_20261026.mjs`, `build_es_features_20261026.mjs`, and `study_es_corroboration_20261026.mjs`.

## Top findings (ranked)

- **S1 (High) — the residual doesn't isolate ES.** `r_L = aN − b·aE` is NQ's move residualized against ES, still ~30–40% correlated with NQ's own approach (`sqrt(1−ρ²)`); the prereg's claim it "keeps r_L from being a re-labelled NQ approach size" is wrong. The real isolation comes from the `aN`-tercile stratification + the NQ-only reference row (both present but *not gated*).
- **S2 (High) — hold-out is underpowered for the practical bar.** The 60/40 date split leaves ~40% of days in hold-out, so its CI is ~1.6× wider than the stated ~0.01–0.02 ATR60 → structural bias toward a **false null**.
- **S4/S5 (Medium, UNVERIFIED)** — timestamp alignment is proven only on a 1-in-6 day sample, and the L=60 first-hour lookback depends on overnight (08:34 ET) bars that the `rth_minutes≥371` filter never validates.
- **S6 (Low-Med)** — Family C's OLS doesn't filter nulls the way Family A does; null rows coerce to 0 and land in the bottom tercile.

## Direct answers to your questions

- **Lookahead:** `C[t-1]`, `C[t-1-L]`, `C[t-1-k]` are **safe** (entry `open[t+1]`, features strictly before `t`). Roll handling is **correct** (the `(ts+6h)::date` contract mismatch only ever touches the excluded roll day).
- **Control artifact:** the absolute within-`f` contrast **is** free of the same-day-random-control artifact, but has a residual day-drift-imbalance caveat (CI guards it; point estimate is fragile).
- **Power:** "~15,000 / 0.01–0.02 ATR60" is plausible but only for the *full sample*; the confirmatory hold-out is the binding, weaker stage.
- **Canary at 1-min:** **structurally too coarse.** Family C is a lead-lag claim with a sub-minute timescale and a verified ~0 unconditional 1-min lead; its null would be uninformative and a SHOWN near-certain noise. Family A (co-movement) *is* testable.

I supplied **8 verbatim-adoptable amendments** (residual framing + reference-row gate, hold-out power gating, within-day demeaning, alignment proof on all days + overnight window, Family C null handling, practical-bar on hold-out/P&L, opposite-direction recording, and demoting Family C to exploratory).
